\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}}\max_{i\in[n]}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}w_{gi}\Tr\{\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon/2}\max_{(g,i)\in[p]\times[n]}w_{gi})

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}).

Next, it is straightforward to show that for any
\epsilon\in(0,\eta)
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}}\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})-\bm{v}_{u}^{-1}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\bm{v}_{u}^{-\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-\eta+\epsilon}\lambda).

Since
\lVert\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})\rVert_{2}\leq\lVert\bm{W}_{g}\rVert_{2}
, this implies

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{U}\in\hat{\Omega}}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\{\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}\otimes\bm{A}_{g}(U)-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{v}_{u}^{-1}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\bm{v}_{u}^{-\top})\otimes\bm{A}_{g}(U)\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(n^{-\eta+\epsilon}),

where

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{v}_{u}^{-1}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\bm{v}_{u}^{-\top})\otimes\bm{A}_{g}(U)=(\bm{v}_{u}^{-1}\otimes I_{n})(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top})\otimes\bm{A}_{g}(U)(\bm{v}_{u}^{-\top}\otimes I_{n}),

and for
\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}=\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}
,

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\bm{A}_{g}(U)=

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\{\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\bm{W}_{g}-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\{\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\{\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\}.

Since
\delta=O_{P}(n^{-\eta})
and by Lemma
S8.9
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\{\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\}-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\{\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\}\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(n^{-\eta+\epsilon})

for any
\epsilon\in(0,\eta)
. Next, for
\bm{R}=n^{-1}\bm{C}^{\top}P_{X}^{\perp}\bm{C}
and
\bm{\Lambda}=np^{-1}\bm{L}^{\top}\bm{L}
,

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\bm{W}_{g}=\bm{R}^{1/2}\otimes I_{n}\{p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\lambda^{-1}\bm{\ell}_{g}\bm{\ell}_{g}^{\top})\otimes\bm{W}_{g}\}\bm{R}^{1/2}\otimes I_{n}

such that
\lVert p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\lambda^{-1}\bm{\ell}_{g_{r}}\bm{\ell}_{g_{s}})(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})
by Corollary
S8.1
, which implies

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\bm{W}_{g}-(\lambda^{-1}\bm{\Lambda})\otimes I_{n}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon}).

We also have that since
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})
and
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}-n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})
,

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{s}_{g}\otimes(\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g})-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes(\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon}),\quad\bm{s}_{g}=\bm{\ell}_{g}\bm{\ell}_{g}^{\top}.

For any
r,s\in[K]
, define
\bm{M}^{(rs)}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{s}_{g_{rs}}(\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g})
. Then

\displaystyle\bm{M}^{(rs)}_{ij}=

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{C}}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{j\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{s}_{g_{rs}}/\lambda)(w_{gi}-1)(w_{gj}-1)+\tilde{\bm{C}}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{j\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{s}_{g_{rs}}/\lambda)(w_{gi}-1)

\displaystyle+\tilde{\bm{C}}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{j\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{s}_{g_{rs}}/\lambda)(w_{gj}-1)+\tilde{\bm{C}}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{j\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{s}_{g_{rs}}/\lambda),\quad i,j\in[n].

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bm{M}^{(rs)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\diag(\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k})\{p^{-1}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(rs)}\bm{W}\}\diag(\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k})+\bar{\bm{C}}^{(rs)}\tilde{\bm{C}}+\{\bar{\bm{C}}^{(rs)}\tilde{\bm{C}}\}^{\top}+\bm{\Lambda}_{rs}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\bm{W}_{gi}

\displaystyle=w_{gi}-1,\quad\bar{\bm{C}}^{(rs)}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}=p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{s}_{g_{rs}}/\lambda)(w_{gi}-1)\tilde{\bm{C}}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}},\quad g\in[p];i\in[n]

\displaystyle\bm{S}^{(rs)}

\displaystyle=\diag(\bm{s}_{1_{rs}}/\lambda,\ldots,\bm{s}_{p_{rs}}/\lambda).

First, for some constant
c>0
,

\displaystyle\lVert\diag(\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k})\{p^{-1}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(rs)}\bm{W}\}\diag(\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k})\rVert_{2}\leq c\lVert p^{-1/2}\bm{W}\rVert_{2}^{2}\max_{i\in[n]}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}^{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1+\epsilon})

by Lemma
S8.6
. Next, it is easy to see that
\lVert\bar{\bm{C}}^{(rs)}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2}\leq\lVert\bar{\bm{C}}^{(rs)}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})
. Lastly, since the entries of
\bm{X}
are uniformly bounded, identical techniques can be used to show that

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{s}}_{g}\otimes\{\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\}-\bm{\Lambda}\otimes P_{X}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon}).

Putting this all together implies

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\{\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}\otimes\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})-(\lambda^{-1}\bm{v}_{u}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{v}_{u}^{-\top})\otimes\bm{Q}\bm{Q}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-\eta+\epsilon}),

where
\lVert\bm{v}_{u}-\bm{v}\rVert_{2}=O(\delta^{2})=O_{P}(n^{-2\eta})
for some unitary matrix
\bm{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{K\times K}
by Lemma
S8.3
.

For the remaining two terms in (
S8.15
), we note that for

\displaystyle\bm{S}(\bm{U})=[\bm{B}_{1}(\bm{U})^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{1}\cdots\bm{B}_{p}(\bm{U})^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{p}],\,\bm{T}(\bm{U})=[\bm{B}_{1}(\bm{U})^{\top}\bm{e}_{1}\cdots\bm{B}_{p}(\bm{U})^{\top}\bm{e}_{p}]\in\mathbb{R}^{K\times p},

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\{\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}\otimes\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})\rVert_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{S}(\bm{U})\bm{S}(\bm{U})^{\top}\rVert_{2}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{T}(\bm{U})\bm{T}(\bm{U})^{\top}\rVert_{2}^{1/2}.

Our above work shows that
\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{S}(\bm{U})\bm{S}(\bm{U})^{\top}\rVert_{2}^{1/2}=O_{P}(1)
and
\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{T}(\bm{U})\bm{T}(\bm{U})^{\top}\rVert_{2}^{1/2}=O_{P}(n^{-\eta+\epsilon}+\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})
, which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma
S8.16

(Second and fourth terms of (
S8.13
))
.

Suppose the assumptions of Lemma
S8.14
hold, let
\eta\in(0,1/2)
, and let
\Omega_{\delta}
be as defined in Lemma
S8.13
. Then if
\delta=O_{P}(n^{-\eta})
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\otimes\{\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})\bm{y}_{g}\bm{y}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}\bm{\Pi}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-\eta+\epsilon}+\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})

for any constant
\epsilon>0
.

Proof.

Since
\bm{\Pi}
is a permutation matrix,

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\otimes\{\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})\bm{y}_{g}\bm{y}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}\bm{\Pi}\rVert_{2}\leq\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\otimes\{\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})\bm{y}_{g}\bm{y}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}\rVert_{2}.

By the definition of
\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})
,

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\otimes\{\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})\bm{y}_{g}\bm{y}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1/2}\otimes\{\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})\bm{y}_{g}\}][\{\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1/2}\}\otimes\{\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\bm{y}_{g}\}]^{\top}.

Define

\displaystyle\bm{S}(\bm{U})

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{1}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1/2}\otimes\{\bm{A}_{1}(\bm{U})\bm{y}_{1}\}&\cdots&(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{p}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1/2}\otimes\{\bm{A}_{p}(\bm{U})\bm{y}_{p}\}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{T}(\bm{U})

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\{\bm{P}_{1}^{\perp}\bm{U}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{1}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1/2}\}\otimes\{\bm{B}_{1}(\bm{U})^{\top}\bm{y}_{1}\}&\cdots&\{\bm{P}_{p}^{\perp}\bm{U}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{p}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1/2}\}\otimes\{\bm{B}_{p}(\bm{U})^{\top}\bm{y}_{p}\}\end{pmatrix},

where
\bm{S}(\bm{U}),\bm{T}(\bm{U})\in\mathbb{R}^{nK\times pK}
and
\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\otimes\{\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})\bm{y}_{g}\bm{y}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}=\bm{S}(\bm{U})\{\bm{T}(\bm{U})\}^{\top}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\otimes\{\bm{A}_{g}(\bm{U})\bm{y}_{g}\bm{y}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{S}(\bm{U})\{\bm{S}(\bm{U})\}^{\top}\rVert_{2}^{1/2}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{T}(\bm{U})\{\bm{T}(\bm{U})\}^{\top}\rVert_{2}^{1/2},

where
\sup_{\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{S}(\bm{U})\{\bm{S}(\bm{U})\}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-2\eta+\epsilon}+\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
by Lemma
S8.14
. We also see that

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{T}(\bm{U})\{\bm{T}(\bm{U})\}^{\top}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\{\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-\top}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\}\otimes\{\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\bm{y}_{g}\bm{y}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\},

where the same techniques used to prove Lemma
S8.15
can be used to show that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\{\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{U})^{-1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\}\otimes\{\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})^{\top}\bm{y}_{g}\bm{y}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\bm{U})\}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(1),

which completes the proof.
∎

Corollary
S8.3
.

Let
\tilde{\bm{H}}(\bm{U})
be as defined in (
S8.13
),
\bm{\Lambda}=np^{-1}\bm{L}^{\top}\bm{L}
, let
\eta\in(0,1/2)
, and let
\Omega_{\delta}
be as defined in Lemma
S8.13
. Then if the assumptions of Lemma
S8.14
hold and
\delta=O_{P}(n^{-\eta})
, there exists a unitary matrix
\bm{v}=\bm{v}(\bm{U})\in\mathbb{R}^{K\times K}
for each
\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}
such that
\sup_{\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}}\lVert\tilde{\bm{H}}(\bm{U})+(\lambda^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{v})\otimes(\bm{0}_{K\times K}\oplus I_{n-d-K})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-\eta+\epsilon}+\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})
for any constant
\epsilon>0
.

Proof.

This follows directly from Lemmas
S8.14
,
S8.15
, and
S8.16
.
∎

Remark
S8.9
.

We can construct
\bm{v}=\bm{v}(\bm{U})
using the following procedure. For
\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}
, let
\bm{v}_{u}
be as defined in Lemma
S8.3
, and let
\bm{v}_{u}=\bm{A}_{u}\bm{\Sigma}_{u}\bm{B}_{u}^{\top}
be its singular value decomposition. By the proof of Lemma
S8.15
, Corollary
S8.3
holds with
\bm{v}
replaced with
\bm{v}_{u}^{-\top}
. Since
\lVert I_{K}-\bm{\Sigma}_{u}\rVert_{2}=O(\delta^{2})
by the proof of Lemma
S8.3
, Corollary
S8.3
holds with
\bm{v}=\bm{A}_{u}\bm{B}_{u}^{\top}
.

Lemma
S8.17

(First term in (
S8.12
))
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
S8.14
and for any
\epsilon\in(0,1/2)
,

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}(\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}})^{-1}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}).

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we may assume
n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}=I_{d}
. Then

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}=\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}-\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}.

For
\bm{R}=n^{-1}\bm{C}^{\top}P_{X}^{\perp}\bm{C}
,

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}=\bm{R}^{-1/2}\{n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}+n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\}-\bm{R}^{-1/2}(n^{-1}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{X})(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}),

where
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})
by Lemma
S8.2
and
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})
by Lemma
S8.4
. An identical argument can be used to show that
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\allowbreak\bm{W}_{g}\allowbreak\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})
, which implies
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})
. A similar argument can be used to show that

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon}).

Putting all this together implies that
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})
, which by (
S8.7
) in Lemma
S8.9
and the fact that
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}(\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp})^{2}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2}\leq c\{1+o_{P}(1)\}
, further implies

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}(\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}})^{-1}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon}\lambda^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}).

Next,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}=&\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\\
&-(n^{-1/2}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}})\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}.\end{aligned}

(S8.16)

Starting with the second term in (
S8.16
),

\displaystyle\lVert(n^{-1/2}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}})\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}\rVert_{2}\lVert(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\rVert_{2}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\rVert_{2},

where Lemma
S8.4
and the above derivation of the behavior of
\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}
implies that for some constant
c>0
,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}\rVert_{2},\,\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\rVert_{2}\leq c\{1+o_{P}(1)\}

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon}),\quad\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{-1/2}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}})\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}).

The first term in (
S8.16
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}=

\displaystyle\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}(n^{-1/2}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}(n^{-1/2}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top})

where an identical analysis to the one above can be used to show that

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}(n^{-1/2}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}).

Lastly,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{W}_{g}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}=&(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\\
&+(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\\
&+\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\\
&-\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}.\end{aligned}

(S8.17)

Result (
S8.3c
) in Lemma
S8.2
and Remark
S8.7
imply
\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}\allowbreak=\allowbreak O_{P}\{\allowbreak(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\}
. For the third term in (
S8.17
), we see that

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}=(\bm{R}^{-1/2})_{k\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})^{\top}\bm{R}^{1/2}

\displaystyle-(\bm{R}^{-1/2})_{k\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top}(n^{-1}\bm{C}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}\bm{X})(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})^{\top}\bm{R}^{1/2}.

Since
\V\{(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\}
is a diagonal matrix with uniformly bounded diagonal entries,

\displaystyle\lVert\bm{R}^{-1/2}(n^{-1}\bm{C}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}^{\top}\bm{X})(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}\{(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\}.

Next, for
r,s\in[K]
and some constants
c_{1},c_{2}>0
,

\displaystyle\V\{(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}n^{-1/2}\bm{C}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}r}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g_{s}})\}\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}\lambda^{-1}p^{-2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\E\{\bm{C}_{ir}^{2}(w_{gi}-1)^{2}\bm{e}_{g_{i}}^{2}\}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}c_{2}(\lambda p)^{-1},

which implies the third term in (
S8.17
) is
O_{P}\{(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\}
. The
i
th row of the second term in (
S8.17
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}\bm{R}^{1/2}\left\{(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}(n^{-1/2}\bm{C})\right\}(\bm{R}^{-1/2})_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}\bm{R}^{1/2}\left\{(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})\right\}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{C})(\bm{R}^{-1/2})_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}\in\mathbb{R}^{K},

where
\E\{\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}(n^{-1/2}\bm{C}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}r})^{(2m)}\}\leq c_{m}
for some constant
c_{m}>0
that only depends on the integer
m>0
by Lemma
S8.2
. As a consequence, Corollary
S8.1
implies

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[n]}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}(n^{-1/2}\bm{C})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2}p^{-1/2+\epsilon})=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[n]}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2}p^{-1/2+\epsilon})=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}),

which because
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}^{2}=1
, proves the second term in (
S8.17
) is
O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
. We can then express the
i
th row of first term in (
S8.17
) as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}^{2}\bm{R}^{1/2}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)^{2}\bm{e}_{g_{i}}\\
+&\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}\bm{R}^{1/2}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)\sum_{j\neq i}^{n}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{jk}\bm{e}_{g_{j}}(w_{gj}-1).\end{aligned}

(S8.18)

First,

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[n]}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)^{2}\bm{e}_{g_{i}}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2})

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}=n^{-1/2}\bm{C}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top}(\bm{R}^{-1/2})_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}-n^{-1/2}\bm{X}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{C})(\bm{R}^{-1/2})_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k},

which implies
\max_{i\in[\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}]}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}^{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1+\epsilon})
, and consequently that

\displaystyle\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}^{2}\bm{R}^{1/2}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)^{2}\bm{e}_{g_{i}}\rVert_{2}\leq\lambda^{-1/2}O_{P}\{(\max_{i\in[n]}n\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}^{4})^{1/2}\}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}).

Finally, the second term in (
S8.18
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}\bm{R}^{1/2}\left\{(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)\sum_{j\neq i}^{n}\bm{e}_{g_{j}}(w_{gj}-1)(n^{-1/2}\bm{C}_{j\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}})^{\top}\right\}(\bm{R}^{-1/2})_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}\bm{R}^{1/2}\left\{(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)\sum_{j\neq i}^{n}\bm{e}_{g_{j}}(w_{gj}-1)(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}_{j\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}})^{\top}\right\}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{C})(\bm{R}^{-1/2})_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k}.

Since

\displaystyle\E\left[\left\{\sum_{j\neq i}^{n}\bm{e}_{g_{j}}(w_{gj}-1)(n^{-1/2}\bm{C}_{jr})\right\}^{2m}\right],\,\E\left[\left\{\sum_{j\neq i}^{n}\bm{e}_{g_{j}}(w_{gj}-1)(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}_{js})\right\}^{2m}\right]\leq c_{m}

for
r\in[K]
,
s\in[d]
, and any integer
m>0
and constant
c_{m}
that only depends on
m
by the proofs of Lemmas
S8.2
and
S8.4
, Corollary
S8.1
implies

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[n]}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)\sum_{j\neq i}^{n}\bm{e}_{g_{j}}(w_{gj}-1)(n^{-1/2}\bm{C}_{j\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}})^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2}p^{-1/2+\epsilon})=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[n]}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})(w_{gi}-1)\sum_{j\neq i}^{n}\bm{e}_{g_{j}}(w_{gj}-1)(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}_{j\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}})^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2}p^{-1/2+\epsilon})=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}).

Since
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{ik}^{2}=1
, this shows the first term in (
S8.17
) is
O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
, and completes the proof.
∎

Lemma
S8.18

(First term in (
S8.12
))
.

Suppose the assumptions of Lemma
S8.14
hold and let
\bm{s}^{(1)}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}
. Then for any constant
\epsilon\in(0,1/2)
,
\lVert\bm{s}^{(1)}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})
.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, assume
n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}=I_{K}
. We can express
\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}
as

\displaystyle\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}=\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}-n^{-1/2}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g})\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}.

The same techniques used to prove Lemma
S8.17
can be used to show

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g})\rVert_{2}=n^{\epsilon}

for any
\epsilon\in(0,1/2)
, which implies

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}n^{-1/2}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g})\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon}).

Next,

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})^{\top}\rVert_{2}O_{P}(1).

To prove the results, we therefore only have to show that

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon}),

which follows because for any
k\in[K]
and some constants
c_{1},c_{2}>0
,

\displaystyle\E\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g_{k}})\}_{i}^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-2}\Tr\{\V(\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g_{k}})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\Tr[\E\{\V(\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g_{k}}\mid\bm{C})\}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}\lambda^{-1}p^{-2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\Tr\{\V(\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g})\}\leq c_{1}c_{2}\lambda^{-1}.

∎

Lemma
S8.19

(Second term in (
S8.12
))
.

Define

\displaystyle\bm{s}^{(2)}=(p\lambda)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\{\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}-\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}(\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}})^{-1}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\}\bm{e}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}(\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}})^{-1}.

Then under the assumptions of Lemma
S8.14
and for any
\epsilon\in(0,1/2)
,
\lVert\bm{s}^{(2)}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

Proof.

Define
\bm{S}=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{A}_{1}(\tilde{\bm{C}})\bm{e}_{1}\cdots\bm{A}_{p}(\tilde{\bm{C}})\bm{e}_{p}\end{pmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times p}
and
\bm{T}=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{B}_{1}(\tilde{\bm{C}})^{\top}\bm{e}_{1}\cdots\bm{B}_{p}(\tilde{\bm{C}})^{\top}\bm{e}_{p}\end{pmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{K\times p}
. Then
\bm{s}^{(2)}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{S}\bm{T}^{\top}
, which implies

\displaystyle\lVert\bm{s}^{(2)}\rVert_{2}\leq\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{S}\bm{S}^{\top}\rVert_{2}^{1/2}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{T}\bm{T}^{\top}\rVert_{2}^{1/2}.

The proof of Lemma
S8.14
shows that

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{S}\bm{S}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{A}_{g}(\tilde{\bm{C}})\bm{e}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{A}_{g}(\tilde{\bm{C}})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

and the proof of Lemma
S8.15
shows that

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{T}\bm{T}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{B}_{g}(\tilde{\bm{C}})^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{B}_{g}(\tilde{\bm{C}})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}).

∎

Theorem
S8.1
.

Suppose the assumptions of Lemma
S8.14
hold and let
\bm{\Lambda}=np^{-1}\bm{L}^{\top}\bm{L}
,
f
and
\Omega_{\delta}
be as defined in (
S8.1
) and Lemma
S8.13
, respectively, and
\hat{\bm{C}}\in\argmax_{\bm{U}\in\Omega_{\delta}}f(\bm{U})
. Then there exist
\hat{\bm{v}}\in\mathbb{R}^{K\times K}
,
\hat{\bm{z}}\in\mathbb{R}^{(n-d-K)\times K}
, and a unitary matrix
\bm{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{K\times K}
such that
\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{v}}+\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{z}}=I_{K}
and the following hold for any constant
\epsilon\in(0,1/2)
:

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{C}}=\tilde{\bm{C}}\hat{\bm{v}}+\bm{Q}\hat{\bm{z}},\quad\lVert\hat{\bm{v}}-\bm{v}\rVert_{2},\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}-p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})^{\top}\bm{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{v}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}).

(S8.19)

Proof.

The expression for
\hat{\bm{C}}
is a direct consequence of Lemma
S8.3
. By Lemma
S8.3
,
\lVert\hat{\bm{v}}-\bm{v}\rVert_{2}=O(\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}\rVert_{2}^{2})
for
\bm{v}=\hat{\bm{A}}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}
and
\hat{\bm{A}},\hat{\bm{B}}\in\mathbb{R}^{K\times K}
the left and right singular vectors of
\hat{\bm{v}}
. For
t\in[0,1]
, let
\hat{\bm{z}}(t)=t\hat{\bm{z}}
,
\hat{\bm{v}}(t)=\bm{v}\{I_{K}-\hat{\bm{z}}(t)^{\top}\hat{\bm{z}}(t)\}^{1/2}
, and
\bm{\gamma}(t)=(\hat{\bm{v}}(t)^{\top},\,\hat{\bm{z}}(t)^{\top})^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{(n-d)\times K}
. Since
\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{v}}+\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{z}}=I_{K}
,
\hat{\bm{z}}
,
\hat{\bm{v}}=\bm{v}(I_{K}-\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{z}})^{1/2}
, meaning
\bm{\gamma}(0)=(\bm{v}^{\top},\,\bm{0})^{\top}
,
\bm{\gamma}(1)=(\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top},\,\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top})^{\top}
, and for
\hat{\bm{C}}(t)=\bm{C}\hat{\bm{v}}(t)+\bm{Q}\hat{\bm{z}}(t)
,

\displaystyle\lVert\bm{\gamma}(t)-\bm{\gamma}(0)\rVert_{2}=\lVert\hat{\bm{C}}(t)-\bm{C}\bm{v}\rVert_{2}\leq c_{1}\lVert\hat{\bm{C}}-\bm{C}\bm{v}\rVert_{2}\leq c_{2}\lVert P_{\hat{\bm{C}}}-P_{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2},\quad t\in[0,1]

for some constants
c_{1},c_{2}>0
. By Taylor’s Theorem

\displaystyle\bm{0}=\myvec[\tilde{\bm{s}}\{\bm{\gamma}(1)\}]=\myvec[\tilde{\bm{s}}\{\bm{\gamma}(0)\}]+\smallint_{0}^{1}\tilde{\bm{H}}\{\bm{\gamma}(t)\}\nabla_{t}\myvec\{\bm{\gamma}(t)\}\text{d}t

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in[0,1]}\left\lVert\nabla_{t}\myvec\{\bm{\gamma}(t)\}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\left\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}\right\rVert_{F}[1+\left\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\{1+o_{P}(1)\}],

(S8.20)

where
\left\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}\right\rVert_{F}=O_{P}(n^{-\eta})
for any
\eta\in(0,1/4)
by Corollary
S8.2
and Lemma
S8.3
. Then by the expression for
\tilde{\bm{s}}
in (
S8.12
),

\displaystyle\myvec\left\{\begin{pmatrix}\bm{0}_{K\times K}\\
-\tilde{\bm{s}}_{z}\end{pmatrix}\right\}=\tilde{\bm{H}}^{*}\myvec(\hat{\bm{z}})+\smallint_{0}^{1}[\tilde{\bm{H}}\{\bm{\gamma}(t)\}-\tilde{\bm{H}}^{*}]\nabla_{t}\myvec\{\bm{\gamma}(t)\}\text{d}t+\myvec\left\{\begin{pmatrix}\bm{0}_{K\times K}\\
\hat{\bm{\xi}}\end{pmatrix}\right\}

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{s}}_{z}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})^{\top}\bm{v},\quad\tilde{\bm{H}}^{*}=-(\lambda^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{v})\otimes(\bm{0}_{K\times K}\oplus I_{n-d-K})

for any
\epsilon>0
, where
\lVert\hat{\bm{\xi}}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
by Lemmas
S8.17
and
S8.19
. Corollary
S8.3
and (
S8.20
) imply

\displaystyle\left\lVert\smallint_{0}^{1}[\tilde{\bm{H}}\{\bm{\gamma}(t)\}-\tilde{\bm{H}}^{*}]\nabla_{t}\myvec\{\bm{\gamma}(t)\}\text{d}t\right\rVert_{2}=o_{P}(\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}\rVert_{2}),

where an application of Lemma
S8.18
then implies
\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
. An application Lemma
S8.3
and further applications of Corollary
S8.3
and (
S8.20
) complete the proof.
∎

Corollary
S8.4
.

Suppose the assumptions of Theorem
S8.1
hold. Then the conclusions of Theorem
5.1
hold.

Proof.

This is a direct consequence of Theorem
S8.1
and Lemma
S8.3
.
∎

Corollary
S8.5
.

Suppose the assumptions of Theorem
S8.1
hold. Then
\lVert\hat{\bm{C}}-\tilde{\bm{C}}\bm{v}\rVert_{\infty}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
.

Proof.

Let
\bm{Z}=n^{-1/2}[\bm{C},\bm{X}]
,
\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}=\bm{v}^{\top}(\lambda^{-1}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\ell}_{g}
, and
\bm{\Delta}=\hat{\bm{z}}-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}
. Then (
S8.19
) in Theorem
S8.1
implies

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \lVert\hat{\bm{C}}-\tilde{\bm{C}}\bm{v}\rVert_{\infty}\leq&\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}(\hat{\bm{v}}-\bm{v})\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert\bm{Q}\bm{\Delta}\rVert_{\infty}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gk}\rVert_{\infty}\\
&+\lVert\bm{Z}(\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})^{-1}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{\infty}\\
&+\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{\infty}\\
&+\lVert\bm{Z}(\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})^{-1}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{\infty},\end{aligned}

(S8.21)

where since
\tilde{\bm{C}}(\hat{\bm{v}}-\bm{v})
and
\bm{Q}\bm{\Delta}
are at most rank
2K
,

\displaystyle\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}(\hat{\bm{v}}-\bm{v})\rVert_{\infty}=O\{\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}(\hat{\bm{v}}-\bm{v})\rVert_{2}\}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon}),\quad\lVert\bm{Q}\bm{\Delta}\rVert_{\infty}=O(\lVert\bm{Q}\bm{\Delta}\rVert_{2})=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

for any
\epsilon>0
by Theorem
S8.1
. Similarly, since the fourth and sixth matrices to the right of the inequality in (
S8.21
) are at most rank
K
,

\displaystyle\lVert\bm{Z}(\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})^{-1}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{\infty}=O_{P}\{\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}\}

\displaystyle\lVert\bm{Z}(\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})^{-1}(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\{\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}\}.

To derive the asymptotic properties of these Euclidean norms, we first see that for some constants
c_{1},c_{2}>0
and
\tilde{\bm{c}}_{i}=(\bm{C}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top},\bm{X}_{i\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top})^{\top}
,

\displaystyle(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}=(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}+(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}

\displaystyle\V\{(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gk}\}\leq c_{1}p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\E\{(w_{gi}-1)^{2}\bm{e}_{gi}^{2}\tilde{\bm{c}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{c}}_{i}^{\top}\}\preceq c_{2}I_{K},\quad k\in[K],

where
\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(1)
by Lemma
S8.2
. This implies the fourth term in (
S8.21
) is
O_{P}(\lambda^{-1})
. Next, Lemma
S8.4
and Corollary
S8.1
imply that for some constant
c>0
and any
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})\rVert_{2}=

\displaystyle O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon}),\quad\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\{\rVert(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\}_{2}\leq c\{1+o_{P}(1)\}

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert(\lambda n)^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\rVert_{2}\leq

\displaystyle c\{\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}+\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(n^{\epsilon}),

which implies the sixth term in (
S8.21
) is
O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
. We next consider the third term in (
S8.21
). For
i\in[n]
and
k\in[K]
, the
i
th element of the third vector can be expressed as

\displaystyle x_{ik}=p^{-1}\lambda^{-1/2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}w_{gi}\bm{e}_{gi}(\lambda^{-1/2}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gk})=p^{-1/2}\lambda^{-1/2}(p^{-1/2}\sum_{g=1}^{p}a_{gi}b_{gk})

\displaystyle a_{gi}=w_{gi}\bm{e}_{gi},\quad\lvert b_{gk}\rvert\leq c

for some constant
c>0
. Since
a_{1i},\ldots,a_{pi}
are independent and mean 0, Lemma
S8.4
and Corollary
S8.1
imply
\max_{i\in[n]}\lvert x_{ik}\rvert=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
, which implies the third term in (
S8.21
) is
O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
. For the fifth and final term in (
S8.21
), assume without loss of generality that
n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}=I_{d}
. Then the fifth term in (
S8.21
) can be bounded above by

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})\{(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}-I_{d}\}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{\infty}\\
&+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}j})(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}j})^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gk}^{\top}\rVert_{\infty}.\end{aligned}

(S8.22)

First, since the first matrix is at most rank
K
, and
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}-I_{K}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})\{(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}-I_{K}\}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})\{(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}-I_{K}\}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

for some constant
c>0
. Next, for fixed
j\in[d]
and
k\in[K]
, the
i
th element of the second matrix in (
S8.22
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle(\lambda n)^{-1/2}p^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}(w_{gi}-1)\bm{X}_{ij}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}j}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g})(\lambda^{-1/2}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gk})=(\lambda n)^{-1/2}a_{ijk},\quad i\in[n],

where for some constant
c>0
,

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[n]}\lvert a_{ijk}\rvert\leq c(\max_{i\in[n],g\in[p]}\lvert w_{gi}-1\rvert)(\max_{g\in[p]}\lvert n^{-1/2}\bm{X}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}j}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\rvert)=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})

for some any constant
\epsilon>0
, which completes the proof.
∎

Corollary
S8.6
.

Suppose the assumptions of Theorem
S8.1
hold and let
\hat{\bm{C}}
and
\bm{v}
be as defined in the statement of Theorem
S8.1
. Then for any
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})(\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\bm{v})^{-1}-I_{K}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

(S8.23a)

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}}-I_{K}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon}).

(S8.23b)

Proof.

Let
\bm{A}_{g}=\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\bm{v}
and
\hat{\bm{v}}
,
\hat{\bm{z}}
be as defined in Theorem
S8.1
. We can express
\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}}
as

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}}=

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\hat{\bm{v}}+\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\hat{\bm{v}}+(\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\hat{\bm{v}})^{\top}+\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{Q}\hat{\bm{z}}.

By (
S8.7
) in Lemma
S8.9
,
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2}=1+O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\left\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{Q}\hat{\bm{z}}\bm{A}_{g}^{-1}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\left\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{A}_{g}^{-1}\rVert_{2}\max_{g\in[p]}\left\lVert\bm{W}_{g}\right\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

for any
\epsilon>0
by Theorem
S8.1
. Next,

\displaystyle\lVert\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\hat{\bm{v}}\bm{A}_{g}^{-1}-I_{K}\rVert_{2}\leq 2\left\lVert\hat{\bm{v}}-\bm{v}\right\rVert_{2}\lVert\bm{A}_{g}\rVert_{2}\lVert\bm{A}_{g}^{-1}\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\hat{\bm{v}}-\bm{v}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\lVert\bm{A}_{g}^{-1}\rVert_{2}\lVert\bm{A}_{g}\rVert_{2}.

Since
\left\lVert\hat{\bm{v}}-\bm{v}\right\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
by Theorem
S8.1
,
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\hat{\bm{v}}\bm{A}_{g}^{-1}-I_{K}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
. Next, let
\bm{s}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}(n^{1/2}\bm{\ell}_{g})^{\top}(\lambda^{-1}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}
. Then

\displaystyle\lVert\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\hat{\bm{v}}\rVert_{2}\leq\lVert\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{Q}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2}+\lVert\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{Q}-\hat{\bm{z}}\rVert_{2}\lVert\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2},

where
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2}\leq c\{1+o_{P}(1)\}
for some constant
c>0
by the proof of Lemma
S8.9
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}(\lVert\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{Q}-\hat{\bm{z}}\rVert_{2}\lVert\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2})\leq c\{1+o_{P}(1)\}\lVert\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{Q}-\hat{\bm{z}}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

by Theorem
S8.1
. Since an application of Lemma
S8.9
and (
S8.23a
) imply (
S8.23b
), we need only show that
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{Q}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
to complete the proof.

Define
\bm{H}=\bm{Q}\bm{Q}^{\top}=P_{[\bm{X},\tilde{\bm{C}}]}^{\perp}
. Then for any
r,t\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\bm{s}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}r}^{\top}\bm{Q}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gr}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}+(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{P}_{h}^{\perp}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\\
&\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}=n^{1/2}\lambda^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{r\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}}^{\top}\bm{\ell}_{h},\quad h\in[p],\end{aligned}

(S8.24)

where
\lvert\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\rvert\leq c\lambda^{1/2}
for some constant
c>0
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lvert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gr}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\rvert\leq c(\lambda n)^{-1/2}(\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2})(\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{W}_{g}^{2}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\rVert_{2})

for some constant
c>0
. It is easy to see that
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})
and
\max_{g\in[p]}\allowbreak\lVert\bm{W}_{g}^{2}\allowbreak\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(1)
, where the latter follows from Corollary
S8.1
and implies

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lvert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gr}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\rvert=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

for any
\epsilon>0
. For the second term in (
S8.24
),

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{P}_{h}^{\perp}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\\
&-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\\
&-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\\
&+(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\{\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\\
&\times\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\}.\end{aligned}

(S8.25)

Define
\bm{R}=(n^{-1}\bm{C}^{\top}P_{\bm{X}}^{\perp}\bm{C})^{-1/2}
and
\bm{Z}=[\bm{C},\bm{X}]
, where we assume without loss of generality that
n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}=I_{d}
. Then the first term in (
S8.25
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{C})\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\\
&+(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{C})\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\\
&-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t})\\
&-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{X})(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t})\\
&-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}(n^{-1/2}\bm{Z})(n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})\{n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})P_{\bm{X}}^{\perp}\bm{C}\}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}.\end{aligned}

(S8.26)

Define
\bm{x}_{g}=(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}
. Since
\lvert\lambda^{-1/2}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\rvert\leq c
for some constant
c>0
,
\E(\bm{x}_{g_{i}}^{2m})\leq c_{m}
for all
i\in[n]
for some constant
c_{m}>0
that only depends on the positive integer
m
by Lemma
S8.4
. Since the rows of
(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{C})
are mean
\bm{0}
and independent conditional on
\{\bm{C},\bm{x}_{g}\}
, Corollary
S8.1
implies

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lvert(\lambda p)^{-1/2}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{C}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}k})\rvert=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon}),\quad k\in[K]

for any
\epsilon>0
, which then implies

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lvert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})(n^{-1/2}\bm{C})\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\rvert=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

for any
\epsilon>0
. Identical analyses and repeated applications of Lemma
S8.4
and Corollary
S8.1
can be used to show that the maximum, over
g\in[p]
, absolute value of the remaining four terms in (
S8.26
) are all
O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
, which shows that the maximum, over
g\in[p]
, absolute value of the first term in (
S8.25
) is
O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
. For the second term in (
S8.25
), we see that for
h\neq g
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}=n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\\
&-\{n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}\}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t})\\
&-\{n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{Z}\}(n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})\{n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\}\\
&+\{n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{Z}\}(n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})\{n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}\}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}).\end{aligned}

(S8.27)

Since the diagonal entries of
(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})
are independent and mean 0, Corollary
S8.1
implies the terms in (
S8.27
) satisfy

\displaystyle\max_{g\neq h\in[p]\times[p]}\lVert n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})

\displaystyle\max_{g\neq h\in[p]\times[p]}\lVert\{n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}\}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})

\displaystyle\max_{g\neq h\in[p]\times[p]}\lVert\{n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{Z}\}(n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})\{n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1+\epsilon})

\displaystyle\max_{g\neq h\in[p]\times[p]}\lVert\{n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{Z}\}(n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})\{n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}\}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{C}\bm{R}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1+\epsilon}),

which implies
\max_{g\neq h\in[p]\times[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
. Next, for some constant
c>0
,

\displaystyle\lVert\lambda^{-1/2}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X}\rVert_{2}\leq c\{\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{X}\rVert_{2}+\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{X}\rVert_{2}\},

where, for any
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\max_{h\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{X}\rVert_{2},\,\max_{h\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{X}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})

by Corollary
S8.1
and because
\bm{e}_{h}
is sub-Gaussian, respectively. Therefore, the second term in (
S8.25
) satisfies

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lvert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\tilde{\bm{C}}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}t}\rvert

\displaystyle=O_{P}\{(\lambda n)^{-1/2+\epsilon}\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})

for any
\epsilon>0
. Identical techniques to those used to derive the properties of the second term in (
S8.25
) can also be used to show that the maximum, over
g\in[p]
, absolute values of the third and fourth terms in (
S8.25
) are
O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
. The details have been omitted.
∎

Corollary
S8.7
.

Suppose the assumptions of Theorem
S8.1
hold, let
\hat{\bm{Z}}=[\hat{\bm{C}},\bm{X}]
for
\hat{\bm{C}}
as defined in the statement of Theorem
S8.1
, and define
\hat{\bm{\ell}}_{g}
to be the first
K
elements of the
K+d
vector
(\hat{\bm{Z}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\hat{\bm{Z}})^{-1}\hat{\bm{Z}}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{y}_{g}
. Then
\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\hat{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\hat{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}-(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\bm{v}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
and
\lVert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\hat{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}-(\lambda p)^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\sum_{g=1}^{p}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}^{\top}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
and
\bm{v}
as defined in the statement of Theorem
S8.1
.

Proof.

Let
\hat{\bm{v}}
and
\hat{\bm{z}}
be as defined in Theorem
S8.1
, and let
\bm{s}
and
\bm{H}
be as defined in the proof of Corollary
S8.6
. We can express
\hat{\bm{\ell}}_{g}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \hat{\bm{\ell}}_{g}=&(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{y}_{g}=(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}+(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\\
&+(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}+(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\hat{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\\
=&\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}+\{(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}(\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}\bm{v})(\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\bm{v})-I_{K}\}\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\\
&+\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}+\bm{v}^{\top}\{(\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}})^{-1}-I_{K}\}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\\
&+\{(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}-(\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\bm{v})^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\\
&+(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}+(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\\
&+(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}+\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\\
&+\{(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}-I_{K}\}\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\end{aligned}

(S8.28)

for
\bm{\Delta}=\hat{\bm{z}}-\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{s}\bm{v}
. Lemmas
S8.2
and
S8.9
, Theorem
S8.1
, and Corollary
S8.6
imply

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\{(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}(\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}\bm{v})(\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\bm{v})-I_{K}\}\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})\\
&\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})\\
&\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{v}^{\top}\{(\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}})^{-1}-I_{K}\}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})\\
&\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\{(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}-(\bm{v}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\bm{v})^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})\\
&\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})\\
&\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-\delta})\\
&\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\{(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}-I_{K}\}\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\bm{Q}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1+\epsilon})\end{aligned}

(S8.29)

for
\delta>0
sufficiently small and any
\epsilon>0
. The second line follows from the fact that for
\bm{R}=(n^{-1}\bm{C}^{\top}P_{\bm{X}}^{\perp}\bm{C})^{-1/2}
,

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}=

\displaystyle\bm{R}(n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}P_{\bm{X}}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g})+\bm{R}\{n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\}

\displaystyle-\bm{R}(n^{-1}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{X})(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X})\{n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\}

\displaystyle-\{n^{-1/2}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}\}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}),

where

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{R}(n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}P_{\bm{X}}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}P_{\bm{X}}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2})=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{C}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2},\,\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})

by Lemma
S8.2
and Corollary
S8.1
, respectively, and because

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\{n^{-1/2}\tilde{\bm{C}}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}\}(n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g})\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{-1/2+\epsilon})

for any
\epsilon>0
. Identical techniques used to prove Corollary
S8.6
can also be used to show

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\tilde{\bm{C}}\tilde{\bm{\ell}}_{g}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})

(S8.30)

for any
\epsilon>0
. Lastly,
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert(\hat{\bm{C}}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\hat{\bm{C}})^{-1}\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}\leq\{1+o_{P}(1)\}\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert\bm{s}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rVert_{2}
, where for
\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}
as defined in (
S8.24
),

\displaystyle\bm{s}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\displaystyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\textstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptstyle*$}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{1.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}}r}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gr}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}+(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{P}_{h}^{\perp}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g},\quad r\in[K].

(S8.31)

We first see that for some constant
c>0
, where

\displaystyle\lvert(\lambda p)^{-1}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{gr}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}\rvert\leq c\lambda^{-1/2}\{\max_{g\in[p]}(p^{-1}\bm{e}_{g}^{\top}\bm{e}_{g})\}(\max_{g\in[p],i\in[n]}w_{gi}^{2})=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})

for any
\epsilon>0
. For
\bm{Z}=[\bm{C},\bm{X}]
, the second term in (
S8.31
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{P}_{h}^{\perp}\bm{H}\bm{P}_{g}^{\perp}\bm{e}_{g}=(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\\
&-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}(n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{Z})(n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})(n^{-1/2}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g})\\
&-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\\
&-(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{H}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}\\
&+(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{X})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{H}(\bm{W}_{g}-I_{n})\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{X})^{-1}\\
&\times\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g}.\end{aligned}

(S8.32)

First,
\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{Z}\rVert_{2}\leq\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{Z}\rVert_{2}+\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{Z}\rVert_{2}
, where
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{Z}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})
by Lemma
S8.2
and Corollary
S8.1
implies
\max_{g\in[p]}\lVert n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}(\bm{W}_{h}-I_{n})\bm{Z}\rVert_{2}=O_{P}(n^{\epsilon})
for any
\epsilon>0
. Therefore, the second term in (
S8.32
) satisfies

\displaystyle\max_{g\in[p]}\lvert(\lambda p)^{-1}\sum_{h\neq g}\bar{\bm{\ell}}_{hr}(n^{-1/2}\bm{e}_{h}^{\top}\bm{W}_{h}\bm{Z})(n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z})(n^{-1/2}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{W}_{g}\bm{e}_{g})\rvert=O_{P}(\lambda^{-1/2+\epsilon})

for any
\epsilon>0
. Identical analyses can be used to show that the the maxima, over
g\in[p]
